Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on genomics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 660. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 459 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by genomics generators.
